**[00:09:50.370] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000153)

**[00:09:52.010] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000154)  
So lumalabas po, Madam Chair,

**[00:09:54.590] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000155)  
hindi totoo yung pangako. Ito yung pangako

**[00:09:59.054] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000156)  
ng administration sa pag-provide ng jobs. Kasi dapat natin itanong, hindi lang dapat lumilikha ng trabaho,

**[00:10:05.730] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000157)

**[00:10:11.650] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000158)  
dun sa mga datos na nakikita natin, eh walang quality jobs na nakikreate. Mostly retail trade at

**[00:10:19.290] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000159)

**[00:10:23.490] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000160)  
Kung pwede po bang, kung pwede niyo po bang banggitin sa amin, retail jobs, retail trade jobs, at mga low-paying elementary jobs?

**[00:10:33.010] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000161)  
Dun sa statistics natin, na kino-compile

**[00:10:38.170] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000162)  
natin,

**[00:10:40.090] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000163)  
we make

**[00:10:41.890] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000164)  
a distinction between wage and salary workers,

**[00:10:46.110] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000165)  
and

**[00:10:46.790] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000166)

**[00:10:50.150] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000167)

**[00:10:50.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000168)

**[00:10:53.790] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000169)  
and trade.

**[00:10:56.430] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000170)  
Yung wage and salary workers kasi na classification.

**[00:11:00.770] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000171)  
Or

**[00:11:01.030] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000172)  
type of workers.

**[00:11:02.050] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000173)  
Ito usually na what we consider better

**[00:11:04.830] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000174)  
quality jobs. Kasi dito,

**[00:11:06.570] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000175)  
these are usually in

**[00:11:08.390] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000176)

**[00:11:10.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000177)

**[00:11:13.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000178)

**[00:11:16.770] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000179)

**[00:11:19.490] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000180)

**[00:11:22.050] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000181)

**[00:11:27.850] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000182)  
So,

**[00:11:28.570] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000183)  
and if you look at that data, ito,

**[00:11:30.850] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000184)  
in

**[00:11:32.790] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000185)  
the last 5

**[00:11:34.950] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000186)  
to 10 years,

**[00:11:36.070] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000187)

**[00:11:37.310] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000188)

**[00:11:41.070] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000189)  
In terms of the proportion of those type of workers, in

**[00:11:44.850] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000190)  
the total workforce,

**[00:11:48.070] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000191)  
we can provide you the data to support that.

**[00:11:52.170] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000192)  
Ito

**[00:11:52.730] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000193)  
namang mga sales and trade na

**[00:11:55.810] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000194)

**[00:11:57.950] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000195)

**[00:12:03.410] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000196)  
clerks, half

**[00:12:04.770] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000197)

**[00:12:06.570] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000198)

**[00:12:09.550] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000199)  
yun ang kinakapture ng data natin na underemployed.

**[00:12:12.830] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000200)

**[00:12:18.880] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000201)

**[00:12:22.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000202)

**[00:12:23.730] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000203)

**[00:12:25.270] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000204)

**[00:12:31.530] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000205)

**[00:12:32.640] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000206)  
But I said,

**[00:12:33.470] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000207)

**[00:12:38.650] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000208)

**[00:12:45.230] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000209)

**[00:12:48.630] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000210)

**[00:12:51.750] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-000211)

**[00:13:12.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000216)

**[00:13:13.130] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000217)

**[00:13:14.190] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000218)

**[00:13:21.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000219)

**[00:13:25.130] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000220)

**[00:13:28.630] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000221)

**[00:13:29.950] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000222)

**[00:13:31.750] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000223)

**[00:13:40.930] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000225)

**[00:13:42.970] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000226)

**[00:13:47.410] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000227)

**[00:13:51.410] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000228)

**[00:13:57.290] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000229)

**[00:14:01.019] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000230)

**[00:14:10.390] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000231)

**[00:14:38.210] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-000236)

Yes.

**[00:17:55.420] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (h2r5-turn-000309)

**[00:18:16.780] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (h2r5-turn-000316)

**[00:18:23.160] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (h2r5-turn-000318)

**[01:36:23.600] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001443)

**[01:38:11.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001469)  
alam po ba natin kung ano talaga ang root cause nitong problema na itong 3.5 billion pesos a day of losses?

**[01:38:24.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001470)  
Yeah, Mr. Chair. As I said, I think the

**[01:38:29.290] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001471)  
underdevelopment of our connectivity infrastructure is key.

**[01:38:37.990] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001472)  
It's so well known that we have

**[01:38:40.010] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001473)  
severely underinvested in infrastructure,

**[01:38:45.830] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001474)  
particularly mass transit.

**[01:38:49.330] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001475)  
So, we should have developed mass transit long ago, but we

**[01:38:55.350] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001476)  
preferred instead to develop...

**[01:38:58.230] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001477)  
highways and more roads. So,

**[01:39:02.890] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001478)  
I think that allowed the rapid growth of regions like NCR and prevented the

**[01:39:13.770] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001479)  
concentration of growth, as well as

**[01:39:18.670] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001480)  
hindering the development of other growth areas.

**[01:39:25.130] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001481)  
There is no...

**[01:39:28.910] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001482)  
reason why other

**[01:39:31.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001483)  
regions, other areas of the country should not grow as fast as Metro Manila if we build a proper ecosystem for these areas. So, the rapid migration to Metro Manila contributed part

**[01:39:46.930] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001484)  
of that problem, and then we failed to develop the mass transit that compounded the problem.

**[01:39:53.570] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001485)  
So, the

**[01:39:54.150] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001486)  
trust of our development plan, our long-term vision, as well as the PDP, is to

**[01:40:01.310] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001487)  
build that connectivity so that

**[01:40:04.910] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001488)  
growing areas, rapidly growing areas, are connected with lagging areas so that there will be more inclusive development across the country.

**[01:40:15.910] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001489)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you, Sec. Arsi. I think, in my humble opinion, you hit the nail right on the head.

**[01:40:23.590] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001490)  
It is the in-country migration that is unmanaged. So,

**[01:40:28.650] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001491)  
do

**[01:40:29.150] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001492)  
we have any plan to

**[01:40:31.050] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001493)  
manage the in-country migration, aside from growing the regions?

**[01:40:34.910] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001494)  
Because it

**[01:40:36.170] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001495)  
will take time to grow the regions. In the meantime, people are looking for jobs, so they go to the center, aside from growing abroad.

**[01:40:44.530] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001496)  
So,

**[01:40:45.090] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001497)  
in the meantime that we're developing the infra, which will take a

**[01:40:48.950] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001498)  
lot of time, mayroon ba tayong

**[01:40:50.850] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001499)  
plan to manage the in-country migration that really contributed? To the 3.5 billion pesos a day?

**[01:41:00.210] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001500)  
I think we can start with a few areas that have so much potential for rapid

**[01:41:07.730] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001501)  
economic growth so that people

**[01:41:11.210] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001502)  
don't have to come to Metro Manila as a final

**[01:41:15.470] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001503)  
destination for

**[01:41:16.410] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001504)  
jobs. For

**[01:41:18.210] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001505)  
example, I

**[01:41:19.650] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001506)  
look at Iloilo, I look at Cebu, I look at

**[01:41:22.650] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001507)  
Bohol,

**[01:41:25.350] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001508)  
Pangasinan. There are many of those. Bataan, of course, is a high potential, especially once

**[01:41:30.790] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001509)  
you complete that Cavite-Bataan bridge. I

**[01:41:33.950] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001510)  
think Bataan will be a magnet for investment.

**[01:41:38.910] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001511)  
So, I think the good news, Mr. Chair, is that

**[01:41:44.070] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001512)  
population growth has actually come down substantially and fertility is now just 1

**[01:41:50.650] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001513)  
.7, I think, or

**[01:41:52.810] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001514)  
1.9?

**[01:41:53.630] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001515)  
1.7. 1 And.7. so, there

**[01:41:56.470] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001516)  
is now much less... There is less pressure for

**[01:41:59.672] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001517)  
migration than, say, 10 years ago because of that rapid

**[01:42:05.030] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001518)  
decline or population growth.

**[01:42:09.930] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001519)  
Thank you, Madam

**[01:42:11.270] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001520)  
Chair. Thank you, Sec. Arsi. I'm looking forward to

**[01:42:13.950] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001521)  
those

**[01:42:16.830] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001522)  
plans to really control or manage the in-country migration while we're

**[01:42:22.350] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001523)  
growing the regions so that they don't have to go to OFW or

**[01:42:26.910] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001524)

**[01:42:29.730] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001525)  
Next question, po, is regarding our foreign assisted,

**[01:42:35.250] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001526)  
yung mga ODAs. Aside from the commitment fees, ang dami pong na-delay.

**[01:42:43.810] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001527)  
And

**[01:42:44.530] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001528)  
may penalties po ba tayo doon? Kanina sinabi ni Sec. Dec na

**[01:42:49.630] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001529)  
it's lodged to those agencies, the OTR, the BWH. So, they pay or we pay penalties because of the long delays of our ODAs or foreign assisted projects?

**[01:43:03.970] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001530)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:43:05.190] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001531)  
yeah, I did mention earlier that I believe it would be in the implementing agencies'

**[01:43:11.650] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001532)  
contracts with their service providers or their contractors that might include a penalty clause if projects are delayed.

**[01:43:20.650] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001533)  
But the more...

**[01:43:23.930] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001534)  
What's

**[01:43:24.610] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001535)  
more obvious that always happens when a project is delayed is

**[01:43:28.490] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001536)  
that because of the the... time element,

**[01:43:30.710] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001537)  
the project cost will actually go up.

**[01:43:33.590] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001538)  
Rather than... So, in a way, you can call it a penalty, but it's really in

**[01:43:37.390] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001539)  
another form. It's in the form of a cost overrun or additional

**[01:43:43.030] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001540)  
project cost.

**[01:43:44.550] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001541)  
So, we have witnessed that in several

**[01:43:47.150] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001542)  
flagship transportation

**[01:43:50.170] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001543)  
and DPWH projects that when the project is delayed for years, the

**[01:43:55.750] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001544)  
project would actually double

**[01:43:57.650] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001545)  
in cost.

**[01:43:59.090] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001546)  
But

**[01:43:59.910] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001547)  
I believe the penalties might

**[01:44:01.850] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001548)  
be embedded in their contracts.

**[01:44:06.250] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001549)  
Thank you, SecDec. And it would be good to know whether from DBCC or sa mga respective agencies to know the total of

**[01:44:17.330] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001550)  
penalties or yung mga project cost so that we can really study them. Kasi karamihan po ng delays are the right-of-way issues.

**[01:44:29.710] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001551)  
So, maybe we can re-strategize na katulad sa bottom-up budgeting which was asked by our colleagues na we

**[01:44:41.270] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001552)  
propose projects that are demand-driven from the local level that are shovel-ready.

**[01:44:48.570] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001553)  
So, baka pwede rin gawin yun somehow with our foreign-assisted projects para hindi magkaroon ng mahabang delay.

**[01:44:57.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001554)  
Na umabot ng 10 years, 15 years, and even more.

**[01:45:00.190] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001555)  
So, I

**[01:45:01.270] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001556)  
think my time is up.

**[01:45:02.790] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001557)  
Madam Chair, maraming salamat po. Thank you, Sec. Dec.

**[01:45:05.270] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001557-split-2)  
May I just?

**[01:45:07.870] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001558)  
Yes, please.

**[01:45:08.490] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001559)  
Just very quickly. Mr. Chair, ever

**[01:45:11.330] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001560)  
since I moved to the Department of Finance, I chaired investment, the ICC.

**[01:45:17.570] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001561)  
All the projects now that are coming up for approval,

**[01:45:20.710] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001562)  
we actually now require that they secure the right-of-way first

**[01:45:25.170] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001563)  
because this has become, as you correctly pointed out,

**[01:45:29.090] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001564)  
this is the perennial problem of

**[01:45:31.510] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001565)  
the infrastructure projects. They

**[01:45:34.010] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001566)  
secure project approval first without

**[01:45:36.650] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001567)  
securing the right-of-way.

**[01:45:38.130] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001568)  
So, now we have corrected that sequence. So, we

**[01:45:41.890] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001569)  
require the right-of-way first

**[01:45:43.170] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001570)  
before the project approval. Thank you.

**[01:45:46.570] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001571)  
Yeah.

**[01:45:47.870] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001572)  
Well, I thank for that strategic decision done by the ICC, chaired by SecDec. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:45:56.310] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001573)  
And siguro doon na lang, if kung meron man lang tayong mabigay na amounts, yung cumulative delays.

**[01:46:04.390] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001574)  
Maybe the committee can receive that for the benefit

**[01:46:09.230] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001575)  
of the members.

**[01:46:10.490] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001576)  
Thank you, SecDec.

**[01:46:13.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-001577)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:46:16.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (h2r5-turn-001578)  
Next, to interpelate, Representative Percival Cendaña

**[01:46:21.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001579)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Good afternoon, everyone.

**[01:46:24.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001580)  
Madam Chair, before we discuss the details of the budget,

**[01:46:28.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001581)  
I think it is important for us in Congress to understand the fiscal story behind this 7.2 trillion proposal.

**[01:46:37.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001582)  
The numbers before us today are materially different from

**[01:46:41.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001583)  
what DBCC presented to Congress only a year ago.

**[01:46:45.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001584)  
Growth expectations have been lowered.

**[01:46:47.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001585)  
The projected deficit is higher.

**[01:46:50.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001586)  
Revenue expectations have been reduced. Interest payments have crossed 1.1 trillion. And the composition of expenditure appears to be lower.

**[01:47:01.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001587)  
So,

**[01:47:02.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001588)  
our job is not merely to ask whether 7.2 trillion is enough. We

**[01:47:07.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001589)  
need to ask whether the assumptions are credible, whether the priorities are correct, and whether taxpayers are receiving sufficient value for every additional peso government spends or borrows. The question Congress must answer is simple.

**[01:47:24.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001590)  
Given our limited fiscal space, who gets the money? What

**[01:47:29.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001591)  
are we sacrificing to finance it? And what evidence tells us that these are the right choices?

**[01:47:36.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001592)  
To say that the fiscal space is narrow is an understatement. I'm sure our economic managers will share this sentiment. But that's for another set of questions. Let me begin with last

**[01:47:50.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001593)  
year, DBCC told Congress that the economy could grow by 6 to 7 percent in

**[01:47:56.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001594)  
2027. You

**[01:47:58.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001595)  
are now projecting only 5 to 6

**[01:48:01.279] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001596)  
percent. What specifically changed? And I'll also throw in my follow-up question because of the limited time. How much of the downgrade is attributable to external conditions and how much reflects domestic factors such as weak investment, agricultural performance, infrastructure implementation, consumption, or government underspending?

**[01:48:22.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001597)  
If growth is only 4 percent in 2027, what happens to revenue, the deficit, and the debt-to-GDP ratio?

**[01:49:02.090] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001598)  
Compared to last year, who

**[01:49:04.570] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001599)  
would have expected that we'd have a

**[01:49:06.490] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001600)  
Middle East conflict?

**[01:49:08.550] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001601)  
Who would have expected that global trade

**[01:49:12.130] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001602)  
uncertainty would

**[01:49:13.390] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001603)  
suddenly skyrocket because of these

**[01:49:18.550] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001604)  
changes

**[01:49:20.130] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001605)  
in tariff by the

**[01:49:23.750] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001606)  
U.S. government, for example?

**[01:49:25.190] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001607)  
And

**[01:49:25.890] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001608)  
all these geopolitical tensions that are happening.

**[01:49:29.710] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001609)  
So we needed to

**[01:49:33.010] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001610)  
make our growth targets realistic and our

**[01:49:37.990] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001611)  
approach to addressing our problems more realistic. We believe that even

**[01:49:45.330] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001612)  
though the growth is less next year compared to what we

**[01:49:50.430] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001613)  
targeted earlier,

**[01:49:53.070] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001614)  
this kind of growth is going to be one

**[01:49:56.830] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001615)  
that is enduring, meaning that

**[01:50:00.490] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001616)  
while

**[01:50:01.310] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001617)  
it's lower, we are building a stronger foundation for a faster growth

**[01:50:06.190] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001618)  
in the succeeding years. We can't be irresponsible by

**[01:50:10.970] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001619)  
trying

**[01:50:11.890] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001620)  
to drive high growth when the fundamentals will

**[01:50:15.110] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001621)  
not support it. That can lead us to even worse problems like the

**[01:50:21.050] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001622)  
deficit that you just mentioned, Mr.

**[01:50:25.030] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-001623)  
Chair.

**[01:50:30.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001624)  
Is there a response to my last question about if the growth is only 4 percent in 2027, what

**[01:50:37.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001625)  
happens to revenue, the deficit,

**[01:50:39.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001626)  
and the debt-to-GDP ratio?

**[01:50:42.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (h2r5-turn-001627)  
The DOF, perhaps.

**[01:50:45.790] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001628)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:50:48.510] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001629)  
I think the answer to your question is

**[01:50:52.510] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001630)  
if the GDP assumptions are reduced,

**[01:50:56.450] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001631)  
the assumptions for tax revenues will also

**[01:51:00.870] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001632)  
be reduced as well,

**[01:51:02.190] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001633)  
proportionately to the reduction

**[01:51:05.670] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-001634)  
or changes in the macro-economic assumptions. So, Your Honor, if I may just point out there are several macroeconomic assumptions that we that

**[03:37:31.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003186)

**[03:37:34.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003187)

**[03:37:37.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003188)

**[03:37:45.660] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003189)

**[03:37:51.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003190)  
local government units. Ito po yung reason, Mr.

**[03:37:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003191)  
Chair, Your Honor,

**[03:37:56.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003192)

**[03:37:58.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003193)

**[03:38:04.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003194)

**[03:38:07.440] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003195)

**[03:38:19.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (h2r5-turn-003198)

**[03:38:41.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003201)

**[03:40:37.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-003241-split-2)  
Ah

**[03:40:37.480] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-003242)  
Sec. Kim

**[03:40:38.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003243)

**[03:40:56.020] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003245)

**[03:40:57.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003246)

**[03:41:01.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003247)

**[03:41:11.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003248)

**[03:41:17.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003249)

**[03:41:21.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003250)

**[03:41:24.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003252)

**[03:41:29.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003253)

**[03:41:30.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003254)

**[03:41:35.920] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003255)

**[03:41:40.180] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003256)

**[03:41:44.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003257)

**[03:41:50.850] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003258)

**[03:41:58.130] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003259)  
issue is a matter of settling or paying for the right of way, meron po kasi tayong sinusunod na batas. On how government will acquire right of way in the valuation. That's not

**[03:42:09.250] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003260)

**[03:42:10.190] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003260-split-2)

**[03:42:12.030] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003261)  
GAA discussions in Congress,

**[03:42:18.850] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003262)  
DPWH always insisted that only a certain percentage of

**[03:42:24.010] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003263)  
total project costs could be allocated to right of way.

**[03:42:28.250] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003264)  
So now,

**[03:42:29.230] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003265)  
this representation wants to ascertain that

**[03:42:34.290] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003266)  
that's open. Then,

**[03:42:35.710] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003267)  
it's up to the initiative of the

**[03:42:38.170] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003268)  
representation

**[03:42:42.190] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003269)  
what

**[03:42:42.790] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003270)  
he wants to do with public

**[03:42:45.930] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003271)

**[03:42:57.470] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003274)

**[03:43:07.630] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003274-split-2)  
No, no,

**[03:43:07.710] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003275)  
no, sir.

**[03:43:08.590] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003276)  
You misunderstand me

**[03:43:11.030] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003277)  
Is it okay for the representative

**[03:43:13.550] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003278)  
to

**[03:43:15.110] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003279)  
request that his budget for his district has

**[03:43:19.950] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003280)  
an item for DPWH to buy the right of way even if there is no civil works component

**[03:43:28.170] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (h2r5-turn-003281)

**[03:43:30.130] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003282)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair, the right of way budget of DPWH, as we have always proposed naman po, is a lump sum. So, it depends on the

**[03:43:38.710] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003283)  
DPWH. As to the prioritization, kung ano po yung mauunahin po nila. However, Your Honor, Mr. Chair, may we just highlight that

**[04:10:37.990] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003686)

**[04:10:41.970] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003687)

**[04:11:00.050] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003689)

**[04:11:00.650] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003690)

early

**[04:11:09.090] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-003692)

**[05:13:15.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004489)

**[05:13:19.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004490)

**[05:13:23.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004491)

**[05:13:29.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004492)

**[05:13:29.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004493)

**[05:13:31.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004494)

**[05:13:36.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004495)

**[05:13:40.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004496)

**[05:13:42.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004497)

**[05:13:48.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004499)

**[05:13:54.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004501)

**[05:14:02.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004503)

**[05:14:05.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004504)

**[05:14:08.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004505)

**[05:14:12.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004506)

**[05:14:12.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004507)

**[05:14:20.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004508)

**[05:14:31.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004510)

**[05:14:36.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004512)

**[05:15:09.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004515)

ang

**[05:15:12.545] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004517)

**[05:15:19.095] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004519)

**[05:15:26.547] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004521)

**[05:15:31.556] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004523-split-1)

**[05:15:33.210] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004523-split-2)

**[05:15:37.850] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004524)

**[05:15:41.410] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004525)

**[05:15:53.450] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004527)

**[05:15:55.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004528)  
Now,

**[05:15:55.870] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004529)  
tanong ko po, yung confidential and intelligence funds,

**[05:16:00.770] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004530)

**[05:16:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004535)

**[05:16:26.660] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004537)

**[05:16:38.340] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004541)

**[05:16:44.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004542)

**[05:17:01.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004545)

**[05:17:10.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004546)

**[05:17:11.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004547)

**[05:17:17.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004548)

**[05:17:25.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004550)

**[05:17:26.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004551)

**[05:17:33.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004552)

**[05:17:40.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004554)

**[05:17:42.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004555)

**[05:17:47.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004556)

**[05:17:52.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004557)

**[05:17:54.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004558)

**[05:17:56.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004559)

**[05:18:01.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004560)

**[05:18:01.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004561)

**[05:18:25.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004566)

**[05:18:31.320] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004567)

**[05:18:40.817] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004568)

**[05:18:42.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004569)

**[05:18:43.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004570)

**[05:18:50.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004571)

**[05:18:51.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004572)

**[05:18:53.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (h2r5-turn-004573)

[unclear], time is up po.

**[05:21:04.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004603)  
Secretary Balisacan,

**[05:21:05.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004604)

**[05:21:10.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004605)

**[05:21:13.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004606)

**[05:21:15.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004607)

**[05:21:20.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004608)

**[05:21:23.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004609)

**[05:21:30.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004610)

**[05:21:33.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004611)

**[05:21:35.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004612)

**[05:21:39.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004613)

**[05:21:46.370] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004614)

**[05:21:51.750] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004615)  
Mr.

**[05:21:54.150] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004616)  
Chair.

**[05:21:56.210] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004617)  
And of course, taxes could be one. But again, tax

**[05:22:02.510] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004618)  
issues are

**[05:22:04.770] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004619)  
More complex than that, because

**[05:22:11.010] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004620)  
there are broader concerns that we have to also take into account.

**[05:22:17.730] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004622)  
so, for example, given the fiscal

**[05:22:33.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004623)

**[05:22:33.857] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004624)  
use

**[05:22:34.750] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004625)  
and the

**[05:22:35.032] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004626)

**[05:22:38.810] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004627)

**[05:22:52.150] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (h2r5-turn-004629)

**[05:22:58.930] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004631)

**[05:23:07.050] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004632)

**[05:23:15.770] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004633)  
In general, the position of the Department of Finance is that everything is fungible.

**[05:23:22.050] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004634)  
If you want to remove VAT

**[05:23:25.110] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004635)  
from

**[05:23:25.990] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004636)

**[05:23:27.550] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004637)

**[05:23:29.730] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004638)

**[05:23:32.790] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004639)  
we're neutral. But the

**[05:23:35.190] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004640)

**[05:23:41.803] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004641)

**[05:23:42.273] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004642)

**[05:23:42.750] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004643)  
So,

**[05:23:43.110] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004644)

**[05:23:47.370] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004645)  
Yeah, our categorical position is we are not opposed to the removal of any tax as long as there is a replacement revenue source.

**[05:23:55.270] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004646)

**[05:24:06.530] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004647)

**[05:26:08.330] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (h2r5-turn-004674)

Mr.

**[05:33:17.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004782)

**[05:37:59.530] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (h2r5-turn-004830)

Thank you. And

very,

**[05:39:25.570] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004844)  
very quickly,

**[05:39:26.250] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004845)  
as

**[05:39:28.050] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004846)  
an observation,

**[05:39:30.050] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004847)  
palaki ng palaki yung ating population, yung ating bansa, at yung ating gobyerno.

**[05:39:34.950] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004848)

**[05:39:48.870] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004849)

**[05:39:51.530] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004850)

So I'd like to ask, how did it work for us, ito pong CreateMore?

**[05:40:28.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004852)  
Mr. Chair, I actually want to,

**[05:40:30.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004853)  
if I may rephrase,

**[05:40:35.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004854)  
I'm

**[05:40:37.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004855)  
not sure it's not called rephrase. I need to, I think, correct the term.

**[05:40:42.180] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004856)  
The problem was created by Create.

**[05:40:44.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004857)  
So we created CreateMore.

**[05:40:46.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004858)  
So CreateMore solved the problems of Create.

**[05:40:50.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004859)  
Because Create centralized the approval among all the investment promotion agencies into the Fiscal Incentives Review Board.

**[05:40:57.160] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004860)  
In CreateMore, we devolved it again.

**[05:41:00.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004861)  
So CreateMore solved the problems of Create.

**[05:41:03.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004862)  
Devolved up to a certain limit po.

**[05:41:06.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004863)  
Yes, that's right.

**[05:41:07.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004864)  
Pero still,

**[05:41:08.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004865)  
when it's a huge investment, centralized pa rin.

**[05:41:11.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004866)  
Yes.

**[05:41:12.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004867)  
Plus yung incentives,

**[05:41:13.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004868)  
one-size-fits-all pa rin.

**[05:41:16.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004869)  
No, no. If I may correct that also as well, Mr. Chair.

**[05:41:21.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004869-split-2)  
Yes, please.

**[05:41:21.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004869-split-3)  
The incentives in Create, the investor has a choice. He can avail of the Special Corporate Income Tax, the

**[05:41:29.380] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004870)  
so-called SCIT or 5% of gross income earned.

**[05:41:32.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004871)  
Or he can avail of the Enhanced Deduction Regime,

**[05:41:35.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004872)  
the EDD.

**[05:41:37.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004873)  
So it's not a one-size-fits-all. The investor can actually choose.

**[05:41:43.160] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004874)  
Secondly also, the

**[05:41:44.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004875)  
investments are also controlled. Customizable, if your investment is over 50 billion pesos,

**[05:41:51.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004876)  
you can customize it,

**[05:41:53.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004877)  
which we did with Samsung. When

**[05:41:58.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004878)  
Samsung applied

**[05:42:00.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004879)  
for an investment of a 50 billion multi-layer ceramic capacitor factory in Laguna,

**[05:42:05.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004880)  
we approved a customized

**[05:42:08.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004881)  
package for him because his investment was over 50 billion pesos.

**[05:42:12.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004882)  
So it's not one-size-fits-all. I really think that Create more solved the problems of Create.

**[05:42:18.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004883)  
And also, when you talk about the threshold that

**[05:42:20.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004884)  
goes up to FIRB, I think that's probably 5%. That means 95%, the Investment Promotion Agency can approve 90

**[05:42:29.360] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004885)  
to 95% of all

**[05:42:30.720] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004886)  
applications that go through them. And only 5% to 10% go up.

**[05:42:34.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004887)  
So whoever said that,

**[05:42:36.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004888)

**[05:42:41.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004889)

**[05:42:46.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004890)

**[05:42:59.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004892)

**[05:43:07.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004893)

**[05:43:13.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004894)  
Got registered.

**[05:43:15.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004896)  
Relative to our neighbors in the ASEAN.

**[05:43:18.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004897)  
We're behind. Yun nga.

**[05:43:20.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (h2r5-turn-004898)

**[05:43:22.180] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004899)

**[05:43:22.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004900)

**[05:43:40.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-004904)

**[05:58:12.100] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005101)  
I

**[05:58:12.780] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005102)

**[05:58:14.020] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005103)  
position that when you ask for a removal of, let's

**[05:58:19.160] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005104)  
say, VAT, you have to identify another source when it is legitimately imposed.

**[05:58:25.400] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005105)  
However,

**[05:58:26.640] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005106)  
dun sa systems loss kasi, I'll just read this, Sec.

**[05:58:33.500] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005107)  
No consumption, no

**[05:58:35.800] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005108)  
VAT.

**[05:58:37.800] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005109)  
Electricity loss before it reaches the consumer should not be treated as the same manner as electricity actually delivered and consumed.

**[05:58:46.740] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005110)  
Removing VAT on system loss, therefore, aligns the

**[05:58:50.360] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005111)  
tax treatment with the consumption-based character of VAT and

**[05:58:54.200] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005112)  
prevents consumer from paying a tax on electricity never

**[05:58:57.980] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005113)  
actually used.

**[05:58:59.600] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005114)  
So,

**[05:59:00.180] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005115)  
nag-impose tayo ng VAT on

**[05:59:03.140] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005116)  
consumption.

**[05:59:05.280] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005117)  
Ito kasi, they're

**[05:59:07.580] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005118)  
paying already the system loss.

**[05:59:12.500] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005119)  
Kumbaga,

**[05:59:13.480] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005120)  
pinasa na yung sa system loss sa consumer, ninagdagan pa ng VAT na

**[05:59:18.720] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005121)  
hindi naman dapat i-impose because it's not consumption.

**[05:59:22.120] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005122)  
So, this is different from VAT on electricity, only on the

**[05:59:26.960] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005123)  
system loss. So, what is the position now of

**[05:59:30.360] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005124)

**[05:59:34.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005125)  
You can come up with your

**[05:59:37.480] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005126)  
own rules and regulation. And you've cited several times, na ito yung mga VAT exempt.

**[05:59:46.620] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005127)  
And it covers even previous imposition and then

**[05:59:51.240] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005128)  
later on,

**[05:59:52.480] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005129)  
sinasabi nyo na VAT exempt to. So,

**[05:59:54.840] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005130)  
what we're asking

**[05:59:57.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005131)  
is that I

**[05:59:59.400] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005132)  
don't think we need the legislation. All we need is new,

**[06:00:02.860] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005133)  
probably rules and regulation

**[06:00:05.500] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005134)  
exempting VAT on system loss.

**[06:00:10.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-005135)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair. Your Honor, thank you very much. It's a point well taken. With

**[06:00:17.012] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (h2r5-turn-005136)  
your permission, I will ask our BIR Commissioner, Charlie Mendoza, to respond.

**[06:00:22.920] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005137)  
Yes.

**[06:00:23.460] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005138)  
Madam Chair, tama po iyon. Recently, we issued a revenue memorandum circular

**[06:00:29.340] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005139)  
exempting some

**[06:00:31.000] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005140)  
charges from VAT.

**[06:00:33.300] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005141)  
Iyan po ay iyong Green Energy Auction Allowance at iyong Lifeline Subsidy. We

**[06:00:39.040] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005142)  
issued a revenue memorandum circular categorically stating that

**[06:00:42.480] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005143)  
they should not be subject to VAT

**[06:00:44.700] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005144)  
because these are clearly mandated pass-

**[06:00:47.780] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005145)  
through charges.

**[06:00:49.100] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005146)  
So, the current problem po kasi with system loss charge is

**[06:00:53.400] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005147)  
that it is bundled

**[06:00:56.453] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005148)  
with the gross sales

**[06:00:58.699] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005149)  
of the companies,

**[06:00:59.821] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005150)  
of the distributor.

**[06:01:02.066] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005151)  
So, it becomes part of the gross sale and therefore, it forms part of the tax base of the VAT base. That is why it is currently being subject to VAT.

**[06:01:14.180] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005152)  
But we are closely working with ERC on this, Madam Chair.

**[06:01:18.880] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005153)  
Nag-uusap po kami.

**[06:01:21.620] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005154)  
At

**[06:01:22.220] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005155)  
tinitingnan po namin kung paano natin mare-resolve itong concern na ito.

**[06:01:26.480] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005156)  
In fact, as far as I know, they have a draft

**[06:01:29.080] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005157)  
regulation addressing

**[06:01:31.940] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005158)  
this.

**[06:01:32.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005159)  
At ang pagkakaalam ko po is it will go

**[06:01:36.080] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005160)  
through a public consultation next

**[06:01:38.440] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005161)  
week. And then, titingnan po namin doon

**[06:01:40.300] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005162)  
based doon sa regulation na ilalabas ng ERC.

**[06:01:43.520] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005163)  
Pero maliwanag naman po ang posisyon ng BIR dyan kung

**[06:01:46.740] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (h2r5-turn-005164)  
iyan po ay hindi part ng gross sales, kung iyan po ay mandated pass-through charge lang, iyan po ay hindi dapat ma-subject sa VAT.

**[06:01:54.340] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005165)  
So, Sek, this is important, no? Kasi para

**[06:01:58.360] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005166)

**[06:01:59.700] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005167)

**[06:02:02.920] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005168)

**[06:02:05.060] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005169)

**[06:02:07.680] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005170)

**[06:02:10.100] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005171)

**[06:02:13.700] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005172)

**[06:02:14.620] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005173)

**[06:02:18.940] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005174)

**[06:02:19.920] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005175)  
Kasi in system loss, meron pang...

**[06:02:23.520] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005176)

**[06:02:28.720] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (h2r5-turn-005177)
